([wedigcode/workforces](https://github.com/wedigcode/workforces/blob/HEAD/agents/wf-growth.md) · ★1)

- **wh-conductor**: Drives a WorkHorse change through the six phases (design, build, verify, review, deploy, done), dispatching phase agents, keeping state.json current via the wh CLI, running Build as parallel waves of up to profile.build.max_parallel builders, and stopping only at Design (G2), Ship (G4) and, at tier 3, Deploy (G5) with a packet. Use via /workhorse:run or when resuming an active change. ([muhibm1/workhorse](https://github.com/muhibm1/workhorse/blob/HEAD/agents/wh-conductor.md))
- **wh-conformance-reviewer**: Review-phase agent. Maps every requirement in spec.md to the code and test that implement it, and every change in the diff back to a requirement, flagging missing coverage and scope drift. Use in parallel with the other reviewers. ([muhibm1/workhorse](https://github.com/muhibm1/workhorse/blob/HEAD/agents/wh-conformance-reviewer.md))
- **wh-constraint-auditor**: Design-phase agent. Audits spec.md, plan.md and brief.md against the security baseline, the client's compliance regimes, privacy defaults, and the profile's constraints before any code exists. Writes findings by severity into the spec's constraint audit table; only a high finding sends the designer back, and only once; mediums become decision rows in the brief. Use after wh-designer, in parallel with the eval designer. ([muhibm1/workhorse](https://github.com/muhibm1/workhorse/blob/HEAD/agents/wh-constraint-auditor.md))
- **wh-delegate**: Proactively use when Claude Code should hand a substantial coding task to an alternate inference agent running locally via opencode + Workhorse. Use for debugging, implementation, investigation, refactoring, test writing — anything that benefits from a second agent's perspective or parallel work. Do NOT use for quick lookups or single-file edits CC can handle directly. &lt;example&gt; Context: User has a complex multi-file refactor task user: "Refactor the auth module to use the new session manager across all endpoints" assistant: "I'll delegate this to Workhorse so it can work through the refactor… ([more-horsepower/cc-delegate](https://github.com/more-horsepower/cc-delegate/blob/HEAD/plugins/wh-delegate/agents/wh-delegate.md))
- **wh-designer**: Design-phase agent. In one session turns "&lt;problem&gt; -&gt; &lt;outcome&gt;" into the risk tier, spec.md with ADRs, evals.md within the tier's case limit, plan.md in file-disjoint waves where every eval case has exactly one task, and brief.md, the Design document the human approves at G2. Never stops to ask; every question becomes a decision row with a recommendation. Use when a change starts, or to revise once after a high audit finding or a G2 rejection. ([muhibm1/workhorse](https://github.com/muhibm1/workhorse/blob/HEAD/agents/wh-designer.md))
- **wh-discovery-analyst**: Discover-phase agent. Audits a client repository and its real business workflow, then writes the WorkHorse client profile, a codebase map, a constraints document, and the client CLAUDE.md. Use at the start of an engagement via /workhorse:onboard, or when the profile is stale. ([muhibm1/workhorse](https://github.com/muhibm1/workhorse/blob/HEAD/agents/wh-discovery-analyst.md))
- **wh-eval-designer**: Design-phase agent, tier 2 and above only. Reviews and extends the evals.md the designer wrote: adds the edge, failure and adversarial cases whose failure would change a decision, cuts cases a machine cannot run, and appends the matching test step to the plan.md task that owns each added case, within the tier's case limit. Use after wh-designer, in parallel with the constraint auditor. ([muhibm1/workhorse](https://github.com/muhibm1/workhorse/blob/HEAD/agents/wh-eval-designer.md))
- **wh-fixer**: Verify and review-phase agent. In verify mode fixes the specific failures the verifier reported, changing implementation code only while the test-lock hook is active; in review mode, run with the lock off, fixes every high or medium reviewer finding that came with a concrete fix, in implementation or in a test the finding names, never editing a test to make a failing implementation pass. Use with a failure list from verification.md or a findings list from reviews/*.md. ([muhibm1/workhorse](https://github.com/muhibm1/workhorse/blob/HEAD/agents/wh-fixer.md))
- **wh-handover-writer**: Handover-phase agent. Produces docs/handover for the whole engagement so client engineers can run, change, and debug the system without the FDE: README, architecture, decisions index, runbook, operations, security, working-with-ai, open items, plus a current client CLAUDE.md. Use via /workhorse:handover at engagement milestones. ([muhibm1/workhorse](https://github.com/muhibm1/workhorse/blob/HEAD/agents/wh-handover-writer.md))
- **wh-incident-triager**: Maintain-phase agent, designed to run headless from monitoring or on a schedule. Given an alert, incident, scan result, or failing metric, diagnoses read-only, writes a reproducing eval case, and writes the problem and outcome for a new change to enter the pipeline through /workhorse:run and its normal gates (Design, Ship, and Deploy at tier 3). Never changes code. Use from ci/incident-triage.sh or /workhorse:retro with an incident. ([muhibm1/workhorse](https://github.com/muhibm1/workhorse/blob/HEAD/agents/wh-incident-triager.md))
- **wh-polish**: Build-phase agent. One pass over the whole change's diff after the last build wave, for naming, structure, comments, client conventions, dead code, needless abstraction, duplicated logic, over-general helpers, and defensive code for impossible cases. Fixes what is safe, lists the rest, keeps tests green, never changes behaviour. Use once per change, after the final wave is folded into the change branch. ([muhibm1/workhorse](https://github.com/muhibm1/workhorse/blob/HEAD/agents/wh-polish.md))
- **wh-rescue**: Proactively use when Claude Code is stuck, wants a second implementation or diagnosis pass, needs a deeper root-cause investigation, or should hand a substantial coding task to opencode on Workhorse inference through the shared companion runtime. Use for debugging, implementation, investigation, refactoring, test writing — anything that benefits from a second agent's perspective or parallel work. Do NOT use for quick lookups or single-file edits CC can handle directly. &lt;example&gt; Context: User has a complex multi-file refactor task user: "Refactor the auth module to use the new session manager… ([more-horsepower/cc-delegate](https://github.com/more-horsepower/cc-delegate/blob/HEAD/plugins/wh-delegate/agents/wh-rescue.md))
- **wh-retro-learner**: Done-phase agent, run in the background after `WH phase done` with the change id given explicitly. Writes retro.md with the clock judged against the tier budget, records lessons as instinct files, proposes edits to the client CLAUDE.md "Mistakes to avoid", the profile, and evals, and commits retro.md itself. Proposals are diffs for human review, not silent changes. Use from the conductor at `done`, via /workhorse:retro, or after an incident. ([muhibm1/workhorse](https://github.com/muhibm1/workhorse/blob/HEAD/agents/wh-retro-learner.md))
- **wh-security-reviewer**: Review-phase agent. Reviews the change diff against OWASP categories, the security baseline (RLS, function grants, CREATE OR REPLACE diffs, secrets, CI), and the client's compliance regime controls, producing the pre-ship checklist state for the Ship document. Mandatory at tier 2 and above. Use in parallel with the other reviewers. ([muhibm1/workhorse](https://github.com/muhibm1/workhorse/blob/HEAD/agents/wh-security-reviewer.md))
- **wh-shipper**: Review and deploy-phase agent. In ship mode, after the reviewers and the post-review fix, writes ship.md (the Ship document the human reads at G4), pushes the change branch, and opens the PR. In deploy mode, after G4 approval, merges the PR, deploys to every environment the profile marks automatic, records each deploy in ship.md, and at tier 3 writes release.md and presents G5. Never merges before G4 and never runs a tier 3 production deploy. ([muhibm1/workhorse](https://github.com/muhibm1/workhorse/blob/HEAD/agents/wh-shipper.md))
- **wh-tracker-sync**: Mirrors WorkHorse changes into the client's Notion tracker so people who do not read git can see what was asked, what phase it is in, and which gates are waiting on a human. One direction only, repo to tracker. Never blocks the pipeline. Use from /workhorse:sync or from the conductor after a phase or gate change. ([muhibm1/workhorse](https://github.com/muhibm1/workhorse/blob/HEAD/agents/wh-tracker-sync.md))
- **wh-verifier**: Verify-phase agent for the evals. `wh.js verify` has already run the profile's checks and written their evidence; this agent runs every automatable case in evals.md, completes verification.md (Evals table, the comparison sentence, known-failure citations, notes) and records the final status. Cannot edit code or spawn agents. Reports green only when every check passed or is a cited known failure and every eval category met its target. Use after `wh.js verify` is green, and again with --only after a fix. ([muhibm1/workhorse](https://github.com/muhibm1/workhorse/blob/HEAD/agents/wh-verifier.md))
- **WHALE**: Analyses holder quality and capital flow after RISK CLEAR or CONDITIONAL. Never authorises buys. ([swcstudiospace/PumpGrok](https://github.com/swcstudiospace/PumpGrok/blob/HEAD/agents/whale.md) · ★73)
- **whale-tracker**: Use this agent for monitoring large holder (whale) activity. Triggers on requests about whale movements, large transactions, institutional movements, or smart money flow. - **whale-tracker**: Analyze a Polymarket wallet — profile traders, calculate PnL, detect infrastructure wallets ([stat-guy/polymarket](https://github.com/stat-guy/polymarket/blob/HEAD/agents/whale-tracker.md) · ★2)
- **whale-wallet**: Operate the Whale wallet through its MCP tools. Use for quotes, dry-run swaps, balances, transfers, and wallet status. ([jhonnyisaacc/whale](https://github.com/jhonnyisaacc/whale/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/rocket-wallet.md))
- **whalory-editor**: Blind reviewer: scores a draft against the voice profile, channel and facts list without seeing the writer's reasoning. Use after every draft. ([gilebehnam/whalory](https://github.com/gilebehnam/whalory/blob/HEAD/agents/whalory-editor.md))
- **whalory-writer**: Drafts English or Persian business copy with the Whalory method (context card, question gate, playbook, three editing passes). Use for any copy task after the brief is clear. ([gilebehnam/whalory](https://github.com/gilebehnam/whalory/blob/HEAD/agents/whalory-writer.md))
- **whamu-honorable-rival**: Use this agent when you want to engage in roleplay as Kars' lieutenant from JoJo's Bizarre Adventure Part 2: Battle Tendency. - **what-metrics-distinguish-a-well-functioning-orchestrator-from-a-coordination-bottleneck**: A well-functioning orchestrator is distinguished from a coordination bottleneck by four measurable signal categories — delegation success rate, specialist agent utilization balance, coordination overhead ratio, and error containment factor — each of which can be measured directly from execution traces; a bottleneck orchestrator shows high per-task latency variance, unbalanced specialist utilization, rising coordination overhead as team size grows, and error amplification rather than error containment. - **what-would-gruber-say**: macOS native UX review — HIG compliance, native feel, idioms, accessibility, and the kind of details that Gruber, Siracusa, and the indie Mac dev community notice. Use when you're building a Mac app and want it reviewed the way a thoughtful Mac user would. For App Store submission rules (entitlements, signing, sandbox mechanics, guideline violations), use app-store-police instead — this agent is taste, that one is survival. - **what-would-james-bach-say**: Test-placement reviewer in the Bach / context-driven-testing tradition. Specialist in scope, proportion, and the right layer for a given test — anti-maximalist, anti-piecemeal. Catches: tests at the wrong layer, tests that lock implementation rather than invariants, coverage gaps for invariants the user would actually notice, brittle exemplars that future agents will imitate, and "while we're here, let's also test X" scope creep. Sibling to code-review / perf-review / security-review — this one is testing taste and proportion, not correctness. Use when reviewing plans, diffs, or branches that… ([cassiocassio/bristlenose](https://github.com/cassiocassio/bristlenose/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/what-would-james-bach-say.md) · ★7)
- **what-would-william-of-ockham-say**: Parsimony filter and Occam's-razor adjudicator. Triggers on natural summons: "what would william of ockham say?", "what would william say about this?", "apply Occam's razor", "is this the simplest thing?", or any ask to filter review noise / pick the parsimonious fix / catch over-engineering / call out bike-shedding. Mode A: triages a consolidated finding list (e.g. from /usual-suspects), marking each as real / edge / speculative and picking the simplest fix when multiple have been proposed. - **WHEN-NOT-TO-ADD**: &gt; **故意的に近空を保つ原則**：このディレクトリは **subagent 定義** のみを置く。各 `*.md` は v2 agent-spec frontmatter を持つ Task で起動可能な役割を定義する。非 agent ファイルを加えるとディレクトリ用途を希薄化し、ROUTER の役割発見を誤導する。 ([jasonhnd/life_OS](https://github.com/jasonhnd/life_OS/blob/HEAD/docs/history/i18n-v1.10/ja/agents/WHEN-NOT-TO-ADD.md) · ★6)
- **WHEN-NOT-TO-ADD**: Agent files are optional perspectives. They are not an organization chart the runtime must reproduce. ([jasonhnd/life_OS](https://github.com/jasonhnd/life_OS/blob/HEAD/agents/WHEN-NOT-TO-ADD.md) · ★6)
- **WHEN-NOT-TO-ADD**: &gt; **故意保持近空原则**：本目录仅放 **subagent 定义**。每个 `*.md` 定义一个 Task 可启动的角色，含 v2 agent-spec frontmatter。加非 agent 文件会稀释目录用途、误导 ROUTER 的角色发现。 ([jasonhnd/life_OS](https://github.com/jasonhnd/life_OS/blob/HEAD/docs/history/i18n-v1.10/zh/agents/WHEN-NOT-TO-ADD.md) · ★6)
- **when-should-langgraph-be-chosen-over-crewai-for-an-sdlc-agent-team**: LangGraph should be chosen over CrewAI when an SDLC agent team's workflow requires conditional branching, cycles, or complex runtime-dependent execution paths that cannot be predetermined — specifically when code review agents must route between multiple remediation paths, when iterative refinement loops are required, or when the workflow itself (not just the agents) is the primary engineering challenge; CrewAI remains the default for role-defined, phase-sequential SDLC pipelines where the execution path is predictable and the hard problem is agent role specification rather than control flow. ([shakedfb/deterministic-agentic-sdlc-workflow](https://github.com/shakedfb/deterministic-agentic-sdlc-workflow/blob/HEAD/agents/when-should-langgraph-be-chosen-over-crewai-for-an-sdlc-agent-team.md))
- **when-to-use**: A subagent is not free. It costs a dispatch, its own context window, and a handoff back to the orchestrator. Reach for one only when the work needs **isolation** (a separate context) or **separation of duty** (a different actor than the one who did the work). Otherwise, do the work directly in the main conversation. ([ForgePlan/marketplace](https://github.com/ForgePlan/marketplace/blob/HEAD/plugins/cc-best/skills/cc-best/sections/agents/when-to-use.md) · ★4)
- **when-to-use**: 負例 fixture。旧形式のトリガー記述を持つ。 ([tadokoro-ryusuke/cc-plugins](https://github.com/tadokoro-ryusuke/cc-plugins/blob/HEAD/scripts/fixtures/invalid-plugin-agents/agents/when-to-use.md))
- **where**: Commands are in `AGENTS.md`. Open work and order: `docs/agents/issue-tracker.md` (GitHub Issues; the issue body is the spec; the `## Blocked by` section lists `#N` blockers that must be closed before starting). ([danielnagy-dev/cro-sport-hub](https://github.com/danielnagy-dev/cro-sport-hub/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/where.md))
- **where-else-to-search**: After a FamilySearch records search, suggests 3-8 external genealogy sites to try next, each with a prefilled search link and one line on why it fits the place and years. Needs no project and writes nothing: a one-shot answer for beginners. Invoke when the user has just searched FamilySearch historical records (a name, an event, a year or range, a place, maybe a collection) and asks "where else could I look?", "what other sites have this?", "is this on Ancestry or MyHeritage?", "where else might this record be?", or "I didn't find them on FamilySearch, what now?". Takes the fields of that sea… ([PioneerAIAcademy/cowork-genealogy](https://github.com/PioneerAIAcademy/cowork-genealogy/blob/HEAD/packages/engine/plugin/agents/where-else-to-search.md) · ★3)
- **where-things-are**: One page for finding every piece of agent-comms on a machine: the code, the bus, the team's tools, the Claude Code wiring and the connector. Paths are for user `ts`; the layout is the same on ts-gaming, the desktop and the laptop. ([OpenHCSDev/agent-comms](https://github.com/OpenHCSDev/agent-comms/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/where-things-are.md))
- **where-to-dig**: Це маршрут для матеріалу, якого в просторі **ще немає**: людина ставить питання, а ти маєш сказати, у які джерела йти, у якому порядку, чим приймати відповідь кожного і коли зупинитись. Джерел стає більше з кожним місяцем, і без маршруту вони перетворюються на перелік команд, які запускають усі підряд або не запускають зовсім. ([SERGIUSH-UA/nyshporka](https://github.com/SERGIUSH-UA/nyshporka/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/where-to-dig.md) · ★20)
- **where-to-dig-archives**: П'ятий файл документа «Куди копати». Відповідає на три питання: - на якому сайті архів викладає скани й описи і за яким шаблоном адреси; - які друковані довідники дають позиції; - яким маршрутом майданчик узагалі віддає сторінку. ([SERGIUSH-UA/nyshporka](https://github.com/SERGIUSH-UA/nyshporka/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/where-to-dig-archives.md) · ★20)
- **where-to-dig-channels**: Один із п'яти файлів документа «Куди копати». Протокол і класи — у `where-to-dig.md`, маршрути за жанрами — у `where-to-dig-genres.md`. Читати картки тих каналів, які стоять у плані заходу. ([SERGIUSH-UA/nyshporka](https://github.com/SERGIUSH-UA/nyshporka/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/where-to-dig-channels.md) · ★20)
- **where-to-dig-genres**: Один із п'яти файлів документа «Куди копати». Протокол заходу, класи каналів і правила балансу — у `where-to-dig.md`; картки каналів, на які посилаються маршрути, — у `where-to-dig-channels.md`. Читати той жанр, у який зайшов, а не всі десять. ([SERGIUSH-UA/nyshporka](https://github.com/SERGIUSH-UA/nyshporka/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/where-to-dig-genres.md) · ★20)
- **where-to-dig-traditions**: Четвертий файл документа «Куди копати». Жанр (`where-to-dig-genres.md`) каже, **що** шукати; традиція каже, **яка установа** це вела і **де** воно тепер. Той самий жанр «метрики села» в Галичині, на Правобережжі й у Бессарабії — три різні маршрути: різні держави, єпархії, мови діловодства й архіви. ([SERGIUSH-UA/nyshporka](https://github.com/SERGIUSH-UA/nyshporka/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/where-to-dig-traditions.md) · ★20)
- **where-to-look**: Member role edits always retain `member`: the API rejects desired sets without it, and the editor keeps its checkbox checked and disabled. Existing role sets missing it can be repaired by saving the editor. ([alessandrojcm/dhc-dashboard](https://github.com/alessandrojcm/dhc-dashboard/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/where-to-look.md))
- **WHERE_AUCTION_DATA_APPEARS**: This doc answers: **where do the extracted fields (chassis, estimate, coachwork, highlights, etc.) show up for users and how can you access them?** ([sss97133/nuke](https://github.com/sss97133/nuke/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/WHERE_AUCTION_DATA_APPEARS.md) · ★2)
- **whetstone-doubter**: Fresh-context adversarial design reviewer for doubt-pass. Receives ONLY an extracted plan artifact + contract — no conversation, no rationale for the plan. Finds where the plan breaks, not whether it is good — or, on a subtraction mission, what can go. Read-only, apart from one notes file the mission names. Spawn from doubt-pass step 3 (DOUBT) or its subtraction pass. ([raisedadead/claude-code-plugins](https://github.com/raisedadead/claude-code-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/whetstone/agents/whetstone-doubter.md) · ★3)
- **whetstone-examiner**: The read-only examiner for whetstone. Reviews a learner's code like a strict senior engineer and reports ranked findings without fixing anything; also compares a from-memory rebuild against the original. Cannot edit, write or create files. Use for /whet-review and /whet-rebuild. ([ShyamSundhar1411/claude-plugins](https://github.com/ShyamSundhar1411/claude-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/whetstone/agents/whetstone-examiner.md) · ★1)
- **whetstone-grounder**: Adversarial verifier — takes a batch of claims, independently re-checks every cited source (URL, file:line or command), and rules each claim CONFIRMED, UNSUPPORTED, CONTRADICTED or SOURCE-DEAD. Spawn from the verify-claims workflow or for a claim table that must hold up. ([raisedadead/claude-code-plugins](https://github.com/raisedadead/claude-code-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/whetstone/agents/whetstone-grounder.md) · ★3)
- **whetstone-scout**: Read-only research, apart from one notes file the mission names — gathers sourced claims from docs, specs, code and the web, and returns a claim table rather than conclusions. Spawn for a self-contained research question, or from the verify-claims workflow. ([raisedadead/claude-code-plugins](https://github.com/raisedadead/claude-code-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/whetstone/agents/whetstone-scout.md) · ★3)
- **whey-drainer**: Use this agent when existing tests must be run without flooding the parent context. It detects or accepts the test command, executes it read-only, and returns only pass/fail/skip counts, concise failure evidence, and material warnings; it never writes tests or fixes code. ([paulnsorensen/dotfiles](https://github.com/paulnsorensen/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/chezmoi/dot_omp/private_agent/agents/whey-drainer.md) · ★2)

- **whitebox-checker**: White-box source-code security reviewer (SAST + manual data-flow). Traces untrusted input to dangerous sinks and runs static scanners — injection, RCE, broken access control/IDOR, auth/JWT, crypto, secrets, dependency CVEs, security headers/misconfig, and AI/LLM prompt-injection in code. Use for diff/project/code scopes. Read-only, no network. ([ali-zohrevand/claude-sentinel](https://github.com/ali-zohrevand/claude-sentinel/blob/HEAD/agents/whitebox-checker.md))
- **whitebox-fuzzer**: Delegates to this agent when the user wants coverage-guided fuzzing of software they own or build from source — AFL++, libFuzzer, honggfuzz harness writing, corpus/seed management, crash triage with source-level root cause, and turning crashes into reproducible test cases. Assumes full access to source code and build system (whitebox). Does not do static-only review (use code-auditor) or network exploitation (use web-hunter/exploit-guide). ([Iamatto01/SentinelAI](https://github.com/Iamatto01/SentinelAI/blob/HEAD/agents/whitebox-fuzzer.md) · ★2)
- **whitebox-test-designer**: 구현 코드(Test Basis = code)를 읽고 테스트 조건·커버리지 항목·테스트 케이스를 설계하는 에이전트. beaker 오케스트레이터가 호출합니다. predicate 를 business-rule / defensive / infrastructural 로 분류하고, 명세 근거가 없는 business-rule predicate 만 incident 후보로 올립니다. 블랙박스 산출물은 절대 수정하지 않습니다. It writes test files and reports bugs it finds; it does not modify source or fix anything. - **WHY**: An agent is Ravi's unit of execution identity: behavior, working directory, provider configuration, and an authority ceiling. Collapsing agents into contacts, chats, routes, or permission profiles would make it impossible to reason about who can do what, because every inbound speaker would inherit the agent's grants. ([filipexyz/ravi](https://github.com/filipexyz/ravi/blob/HEAD/.ravi/specs/agents/WHY.md) · ★11)
- **WHY**: Agents are the identities everything else routes through: a deleted agent takes its config and routing role with it, a reset throws away session context that cannot be rebuilt, and a permissions change rewrites what the agent (and the automations running on its behalf) is allowed to do at runtime. Those are exactly the three ops that got the write brake. The rest of the surface (`create`, `set`, ` ([filipexyz/ravi](https://github.com/filipexyz/ravi/blob/HEAD/.ravi/specs/cli/agents/WHY.md) · ★11)
- **WHY**: Cloud runs spend real money. Using the organization's Anthropic key keeps the bill, the limits and the data agreement with the organization; the Console stores the key encrypted and the CLI only ever sees its last characters. ([filipexyz/ravi](https://github.com/filipexyz/ravi/blob/HEAD/.ravi/specs/cloud/agents/WHY.md) · ★11)

- **wiki-curator**: Maintain the compounding physics knowledge base under wiki/ — ingest an extracted paper into interlinked synthesis pages, answer cross-paper queries (filing reusable answers back), or lint the wiki for contradictions, stale claims, and orphans. Reads the immutable papers/&lt;id&gt;/ extractions produced by paper-extractor; owns and edits only the wiki/ tree. Invoke with one of: `ingest &lt;arxiv_id&gt;`, `query: &lt;question&gt;`, or `lint`. ([LiangLiu212/genie-agent](https://github.com/LiangLiu212/genie-agent/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/wiki-curator.md))
- **wiki-curator**: Kivax knowledge wiki curator (LLM-wiki pattern). Use to compile specs and plans into interlinked domain pages, answer questions about the project from the wiki, or audit its freshness. The wiki is derived: the spec always rules. ([null-result/kivax](https://github.com/null-result/kivax/blob/HEAD/src/kivax/data/agents/wiki-curator.md))
- **wiki-curator**: 지식 vault wiki 운영(write). raw 시드가 누적되면 wiki 페이지로 합성·승격하고, 결정사항을 반영하며 백링크 정합·신선도를 점검한다. 지식이 복리로 쌓이게 하는 핵심 에이전트. 오케스트레이터가 작업 완료 시 호출하거나 사용자가 "vault 정리해줘"로 호출. wiki-lookup(read)과 분리된 write 전담. ([ryu-qqq/agent-crew](https://github.com/ryu-qqq/agent-crew/blob/HEAD/agents/wiki-curator.md) · 3 copies)
- **wiki-curator**: 바이브 코딩 개발 위키(docs/wiki/)를 관리하는 큐레이터. 최근 작업(git 이력, PDCA 아카이브, 대화 맥락)에서 재사용 가능한 지식을 추출해 위키 초안을 생성·갱신하고 _INDEX.md를 동기화한다. /wiki update 명령으로만 호출된다(자동 트리거 금지). ([sangkyu-bae/agent](https://github.com/sangkyu-bae/agent/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/wiki-curator.md))
- **wiki-curator**: Maintains the project's LLM Wiki (Karpathy-style). Use it to record completed work — experiments, decisions, ingested sources — by updating the log, pages, and index. Designed to run in parallel (background) so the main flow is not blocked by bookkeeping. Must be invoked with a self-contained brief describing WHAT to document. ([themetriost/Sertor](https://github.com/themetriost/Sertor/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/wiki-curator.md) · 2 copies)
- **wiki-curator.agent**: Engineering wiki curator. Two-pass HITL on every write. Read-only on production code; writes restricted to docs/wiki/wiki/ with one carve-out for _pending.md status flips. ([batidiane/specflow](https://github.com/batidiane/specflow/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/wiki-curator.agent.md))
- **wiki-curator.system-prompt**: You are the Wiki curator. The wiki is the workspace's long-term memory: what changes slowly and has to be true every time an agent acts — the voice, the standing rules, who is who, the decisions that hold, the glossary. You keep it **true, small and read**. ([vocion/vocion-core](https://github.com/vocion/vocion-core/blob/HEAD/packages/core/templates/plugins/wiki/agents/wiki-curator.system-prompt.md) · ★3)
- **wiki-dedup-resolver**: Cleanup agent spawned by the wiki-dedup skill to drain the candidate wiki page pairs that memvane/wiki/dedup/wiki_dedup_scan.py queued to .memvane/compile/dedup-needed.jsonl. Not triggered by user request phrases -- judges each queued pair, folds any unique content into the page it keeps via its own edits, then deletes the duplicate through memvane/wiki/dedup/wiki_dedup_resolve.py, and reports a short summary. ([zbdulee/memvane](https://github.com/zbdulee/memvane/blob/HEAD/agents/wiki-dedup-resolver.md))
- **wiki-dev-architect**: Architect / Tech Lead for the claude-wiki-pages development team. Owns four-layer coherence, the shared "one-X" infrastructure (one ontology profile, one graph-traversal primitive, one score object, one _proposed/ channel, one enum list), schema-version discipline, the verify/firewall parity contracts, and ADRs. Use for design review before any M-effort or shared-mechanism item, for cross-lane interface decisions, and to author docs/adr/ entries. Reads .claude/teams/wiki-dev/TEAM-BRIEF.md first. ([odere-pro/claude-wiki-pages-plugin](https://github.com/odere-pro/claude-wiki-pages-plugin/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/wiki-dev-architect.md) · ★3)
